### 在 Node.js 中使用代理：fetch(undici)、axios、got 的配置差异与常见坑

![在 Node.js 中使用代理：fetch(undici)、axios、got 的配置差异与常见坑](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fzcd0e15661bgjemmyw6k.png>)

作者：Leo Lin（林乐），FlashIP 产品经理，新加坡。利益披露：我在 FlashIP 工作，这是一家代理 IP 服务商。 在公司内网或只允许经代理出网的 CI 里写 Node.js，经常遇到这种情况：export HTTPS\_PROXY=... 之后 curl 正常，axios 也正常，可是原生 fetch() 依然直连，got 也直连。原因很简单：这三类客户端各自用一套代理逻辑，读不读环境变量、怎么处理账号密码都不一样。…

### Deriving α (+0.0086σ) and 20 Other Physical Constants from Zero Free Parameters in Lean 4.35

![Deriving α (+0.0086σ) and 20 Other Physical Constants from Zero Free Parameters in Lean 4.35](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fe2tgs2vlqdemglo44riw.png>)

Most "unified" theories hide dozens of continuous empirical inputs behind adjustable coupling constants, Yukawa matrices, and vacuum expectations.

### Show DEV: LinkUp, a random video chat app that matches people on shared interests

![Show DEV: LinkUp, a random video chat app that matches people on shared interests](<https://dev-to-uploads.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/articles/3otvb2z646ytpt1hl2rv.jpg>)

I've been working on LinkUp, an Android app for random video chat, and wanted to share it here. The problem with most random video chat apps: you connect, both of you say "hi", and then... silence. Skip. Repeat.
